## GRAND PRIX LAS VEGAS 2015 (MAIN EVENT #2)

TOP 8 COVERAGE

There’s never been another weekend like this one. Thousands of players around the world joined together in Chiba, Utrecht, and here, in Las Vegas, to celebrate *Magic*. Yes, there was a tournament—two, in fact—of historic proportions. But there were also dozens of artists, cosplayers, fans, art lovers, fathers and sons, husbands and wives, brothers and sisters who came to Las Vegas to play, trade, discuss, and enjoy the game that binds us all.

At the end of the weekend, two trophies were raised. In the second Grand Prix, Scott Markeson, a Minnesotan who cruised through Day 1, also cruised through the Top 8. He was one of three players counting on the Affinity archetype to carry him to victory, and carry him it did.

Markeson will certainly be remembered for his victory, but the memories that nearly 8,000 players—not to mention judges, staff, visitors, and the curious—will stay with us a lifetime. We met the *Magic* community up close and personal. Just 20,000 of our closest friends, hanging out and playing *Magic* for the weekend.

This is the tournament coverage for the second GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #1 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127064245/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390446).

DAY 2 COVERAGE

One Grand Prix isn’t enough to contain the magnitude of Modern Masters Weekend here in Las Vegas. 3,864 players started in the second Grand Prix, and 395 have returned to for more *Magic* history.

Among the top of the ranks of the second event are Mani Davoudi, David Heineman, Gregory Orange, Ellis Thomas, Jonathan Nunez, Paul Xing, Scott Markeson, Kyle Smith, Jasper Johnson-Epstein, Simon Kim, Anthony Crisafulli, and Robert Marquez starting the drafts with undefeated records. Right behind them folks like Hall of Fame player Patrick Chapin, No. 10-ranked Shaun McLaren, and Patrick Cox to make their move for the title.

This is the tournament coverage for the second GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #1 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127064245/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390446).

DAY 1 COVERAGE

One Grand Prix isn’t enough to contain the magnitude of Modern Masters Weekend here in Las Vegas. 3,864 players started in the second Grand Prix, and 395 will return for more of history in the making during Day 2.

Among the top of the ranks are Mani Davoudi, David Heineman, Gregory Orange, Ellis Thomas, Jonathan Nunez, Paul Xing, Scott Markeson, Kyle Smith, Jasper Johnson-Epstein, Simon Kim, Anthony Crisafulli, and Robert Marquez finding undefeated records. Right behind them folks like Hall of Fame player Patrick Chapin, No. 10-ranked Shaun McLaren, and Patrick Cox are making their move to make *Magic* history.

This is the tournament coverage for the second GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #1 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127064245/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390446).
